=== idx: 932 ===
Gold supporting: transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ) is generally observed as more rapid or more efficient removal of certain types of dna damage from the transcribed strands of expressed genes compared with the nontranscribed strands.
LLM supporting:  transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ) primarily targets the non - template ( coding ) strand of dna. this is because tcr is a subpathway of nucleotide excision repair ( ner ) that is specifically activated when the dna damage occurs in the transcribed region of the genome. in both e. coli and mammalian cells, tcr is initiated by the stalling of rna polymerase at the site of dna damage, leading to the recruitment of ner factors and other cellular components to repair the lesion. the repair process involves the removal of the damaged segment of the non - template strand, followed by dna synthesis to restore the original sequence. this mechanism ensures that the genetic information encoded in the dna is accurately transcribed into mrna, thereby maintaining the integrity of the genetic code. however, the exact molecular details and the full extent of the factors involved in tcr, particularly in the context of oxidative damage, remain under investigation.

=== idx: 966 ===
Gold supporting: as deletions of the x inactivation center ( xic / xic ) and / or the x inactive specific transcript ( xist / xist ) gene result in failure of cis x - inactivation, mutations in genes from this region might lead to preferential inactivation of one x chromosome or the other.
LLM supporting:  the transcript responsible for x - chromosome inactivation is xist ( x - inactive specific transcript ). this non - coding rna plays a crucial role in the regulation of x chromosome inactivation, which is a key mechanism for dosage compensation in mammals. xist is expressed from the x chromosome that is destined to become the inactive x, leading to its silencing and the heterochromatinization of the xist locus. this process ensures that one of the two x chromosomes in females is inactivated to balance the x - linked gene dosage with that of males. the xist gene is part of the x chromosome inactivation center ( xic ), which includes other genes and regulatory elements involved in the inactivation process. while the xce locus in the murine xic has been well - studied as a model for x chromosome inactivation, the human xic is less well - characterized, and ongoing research continues to explore the specific mechanisms and genetic factors involved in this essential cellular function.
